- [(3)]{.lawlabel} The Minister shall issue, in English or French, an
  interim statement of compliance in respect of a port if the port
  security plan has been approved but a security inspector has not yet
  conducted an inspection under section 23 of the Act to ensure
  compliance with the requirements of this Part.

- [(4)]{.lawlabel} The interim statement of compliance is valid until
  the expiry of six months after the day on which it is issued or until
  a statement of compliance is issued under subsection (5), whichever
  comes first.

- [(5)]{.lawlabel} If the Minister is satisfied, on the basis of an
  inspection conducted under section 23 of the Act, that the
  requirements of this Part have been met, the Minister shall issue, in
  English or French, a statement of compliance in respect of the port.

- [(6)]{.lawlabel} The interim statement of compliance and the statement
  of compliance are valid while the approved security plan in respect of
  the port is valid.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2014-162, s. 44
:::

#### [Required Documentation]{.HTitleText3} {#h-710934 .Subheading}

**[[372.2]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-372.2 .sectionLabel}** No person shall
operate a port without a valid interim statement of compliance issued
under subsection 372.1(3) or a valid statement of compliance issued
under subsection 372.1(5).

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2014-162, s. 44
:::

#### [Content]{.HTitleText3} {#h-710938 .Subheading}

**[[373]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-373 .sectionLabel}** The port security plan
shall address each vulnerability identified in the port security
assessment and include

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the organization of the port administration in terms
  of security, including the tasks of personnel who have security
  responsibilities;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} the name of the port administration and the name and
  position of the port security officer, including information on how
  they may be contacted at any time;

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} the identification of restricted areas and any
  security procedures, equipment or systems for those areas;

- [(c.1)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a port set out in Part 2 or 3 of
  Schedule 1, the identification of those areas containing the central
  controls for security and surveillance equipment and systems and areas
  that contain the central lighting system controls as restricted area
  two;

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} a description of procedures for and frequency of
  exercises;

- [(e)]{.lawlabel} a description of procedures for the following:

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} ensuring the security of information in the port
    security plan and keeping the records referred to in section 375,

  - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} maintaining security and communication systems and
    equipment,

  - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} identifying and correcting security equipment or
    systems failures or malfunctions,

  - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} communications,

  - [(v)]{.lawlabel} responding to changes in the MARSEC level,

  - [(vi)]{.lawlabel} preventing unauthorized weapons, explosives,
    incendiaries or other dangerous substances or devices from entering
    the marine facilities in the port,

  - [(vii)]{.lawlabel} reporting security threats and security incidents
    to the appropriate law enforcement agencies and the Minister as soon
    as possible after they occur so that an investigation can be
    conducted,

  - [(viii)]{.lawlabel} reporting security breaches to the Minister,

  - [(ix)]{.lawlabel} securing non-critical operations in order to focus
    response on critical operations, and

  - [(x)]{.lawlabel} periodically reviewing, updating and auditing the
    port security plan;

- [(f)]{.lawlabel} a description of

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} security procedures, equipment and systems for
    access control,

  - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} security procedures for the delivery of ships'
    stores and bunkers,

  - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} security procedures, equipment and systems for
    monitoring the port and surrounding area, and

  - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} procedures for security threats, security breaches
    and security incidents, including procedures for the evacuation of
    the port;

- [(g)]{.lawlabel} the identification of positions for which a
  transportation security clearance is required; and

- [(h)]{.lawlabel} a description of the procedures to facilitate shore
  leave for vessel personnel or crew change.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2006-269, s. 12
- SOR/2014-162, ss. 45, 100, 101(E)
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-373-20081215.html "Link to previous version of section 373"){.PITLink}
:::

#### [Port Security Exercises]{.HTitleText3} {#h-710965 .Subheading}

- **[[374]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-374 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Port
  security exercises

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} shall fully test the port security plan and include
    the substantial and active participation of personnel who have
    security responsibilities in the port;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} may include security personnel from vessels, other
    marine facilities, appropriate law enforcement agencies, the
    Minister and other competent authorities, depending on the scope and
    the nature of the exercises; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} shall test communication and notification
    procedures and elements of coordination, resource availability and
    response.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} The port security exercises shall be conducted at
  least once every calendar year with no more than 18 months between
  them.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} Exercises may

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} be full-scale or live;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} be a tabletop simulation or seminar;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} be combined with other appropriate exercises; or

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} be a combination of two or more of the elements set
    out in paragraphs (a) to (c).

#### [Port Record Keeping]{.HTitleText3} {#h-710977 .Subheading}

- **[[375]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-375 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  port security officer shall, in respect of the port, keep records of

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} security training, including the date, the duration
    and description and the names of the participants;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} security drills and exercises, including the date
    and description, the names of the participating marine facilities
    and any best practices or lessons learned that might improve the
    port security plan;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} security threats, security breaches and security
    incidents, including the date, time, location and description, the
    response to them and the person to whom they were reported;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} changes in the MARSEC level, including the date,
    the time that notification was received and the time of compliance
    with the security requirements of the new level;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} maintenance, calibration and testing of equipment
    used in security, including the date and time of the activity and
    the equipment involved;

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} internal audits and reviews of security activities;

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} security assessment information;

  - [(h)]{.lawlabel} the port security assessment and each periodic
    review of the port security assessment, including the date on which
    it was conducted and the findings of the review;

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the port security plan and each periodic review of
    the port security plan, including the date on which it was
    conducted, the findings of the review and any amendments to the plan
    that are recommended;

  - [(j)]{.lawlabel} each amendment to the port security plan, including
    the date of its approval and implementation;

  - [(k)]{.lawlabel} a list of the persons in the port administration
    who have security responsibilities;

  - [(l)]{.lawlabel} an up-to-date list containing the names of
    screening officers; and

  - [(m)]{.lawlabel} an up-to-date list, by name and position, of the
    holders of transportation security clearances.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} Records respecting equipment that is not used
  exclusively for security may be kept separately from the records
  respecting equipment that is used exclusively for security if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the port security officer documents, in written or
    electronic form, their existence and location and the name of the
    person responsible for their maintenance; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} they are accessible to the port security officer.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} The port security officer shall ensure that the
  records are kept for at least two years after the day on which they
  are made and make them available to the Minister on request.

- [(4)]{.lawlabel} The records shall be protected from unauthorized
  access or disclosure.

- [(5)]{.lawlabel} The records may be kept in electronic format if they
  are protected from deletion, destruction and revision.

- [(6)]{.lawlabel} No person shall disclose security information
  contained in the records, unless the disclosure is for the purpose of
  complying with these Regulations.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2006-269, s. 13
- SOR/2014-162, ss. 100, 101(E)
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-375-20081215.html "Link to previous version of section 375"){.PITLink}
:::

**\[376 to 379 reserved\]**

### [Restricted Areas]{.HTitleText2} {#h-711003 .Subheading}

#### [Access]{.HTitleText3} {#h-711004 .Subheading}

- **[[380]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-380 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} No
  person shall enter or remain in a restricted area unless they are

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the holder of a restricted area pass issued under
    section 384 for that restricted area;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a person who does not ordinarily work at the marine
    facility or port and who is being escorted by a holder of a
    restricted area pass;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} an inspector designated under subsection 22(1) of
    the Act who is on duty;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} a member of one of the following groups who is on
    duty at a marine facility or on a vessel at a marine facility:

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} any police force in Canada,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the Canadian Security Intelligence Service,

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} the Canadian Forces within the meaning of those
      words in Part II of the [National Defence Act](/eng/acts/N-5), or

    - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} the Canada Border Services Agency;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} a provider of emergency services who requires
    access to the area for the protection and preservation of life or
    property; or

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} a member of the complement of the vessel in the
    performance of their duties and in compliance with the vessel
    security plan and the marine facility security plan.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} A commercial truck driver who is required to enter a
  restricted area two at a marine facility or port as part of their
  commercial activities may enter and remain in the area to carry out
  the activities if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the driver holds a valid FAST/EXPRES card issued
    under the Free and Secure Trade (FAST) program by the Canada Border
    Services Agency or the U.S. Customs and Border Protection;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the operator of the marine facility or port
    administration informs the driver of all applicable procedures and
    requirements, as identified and approved in the marine facility
    security plan, and ensures that the driver meets the procedures and
    requirements; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the driver follows the procedures and requirements
    identified by the operator of the marine facility or port
    administration.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} A passenger of a cruise ship may transit a restricted
  area two if the passenger uses a passageway that

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} is demarcated for use by passengers in accordance
    with the marine facility security plan; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} is supervised by a person who has a transportation
    security clearance.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2006-269, s. 14
- SOR/2014-162, ss. 46, 100
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-380-20081215.html "Link to previous version of section 380"){.PITLink}
:::

- **[[381]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-381 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  person who is being escorted in a restricted area shall remain with
  the escort while in the restricted area.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} An escort shall remain with the person being escorted
  or ensure that another holder of a restricted area pass acts as the
  escort while the person is in the restricted area.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} In the case of a restricted area two, no person shall
  escort more than 10 persons or one vehicle at one time.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2006-269, ss. 15, 23(F)
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-381-20061102.html "Link to previous version of section 381"){.PITLink}
:::

**[[382]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-382 .sectionLabel}** No person shall provide
access to or assist another person to enter a restricted area unless the
person accessing the restricted area is authorized under section 380.

**[[383]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-383 .sectionLabel}** The holder of a
restricted area pass shall, when they enter or remain in a restricted
area, display the pass on their outer clothing and above their waist
with, except in the case of a temporary restricted area pass, their
photograph or other facial image visible at all times.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2006-269, s. 16
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-383-20061102.html "Link to previous version of section 383"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Restricted Area Passes or Keys]{.HTitleText2} {#h-711036 .Subheading}

#### [Issuance]{.HTitleText3} {#h-711037 .Subheading}

- **[[384]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-384
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (2), a port
  administration or an operator of a marine facility may issue a
  restricted area pass or a key only to a person who requires access to
  a specific restricted area in the performance of their duties and, in
  the case of a restricted area two, who ordinarily works at the port or
  marine facility or who requires access to the restricted area on an
  occasional basis.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} A port administration or an operator of a marine
  facility may only issue a temporary restricted area pass to a person
  who

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a restricted area other than a
    restricted area two, is waiting for a restricted area pass that has
    been requested;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} is a holder of a restricted area pass and is in one
    of the following circumstances:

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} they have forgotten their restricted area pass,
      or

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} they have lost their restricted area pass or it
      was accidentally destroyed and they are waiting for a replacement
      restricted area pass for which they have made a request; or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} does not ordinarily work at the port or marine
    facility and who, in the performance of their duties, requires
    temporary access to a restricted area other than a restricted area
    two.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} Prior to issuing a restricted area pass, the port
  administration or operator of a marine facility shall confirm the
  identity of the person in the following manner:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a restricted area pass to be issued
    to a person who holds a transportation security clearance, by valid
    photo-bearing identification issued by

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the Government of Canada or the government of any
      province, territory or municipality in Canada, or

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} an employer known to the port administration or
      operator of the marine facility; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a restricted area pass to be issued
    to any other person, by

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} one of the means of identification referred to in
      paragraph (a), or

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} other documentation containing sufficient
      information to enable identification of the person.

- [(4)]{.lawlabel} Prior to issuing a restricted area pass for a
  restricted area two, the port administration or operator of the marine
  facility shall verify with the Minister that the person has been
  issued a transportation security clearance as required by section 503.

- [(5)]{.lawlabel} The port administration or operator of the marine
  facility shall confirm the period during which access to the
  restricted area is required by way of documentation that sets out an
  expiry date or other relevant information.

- [(6)]{.lawlabel} The port administration or operator of the marine
  facility shall issue restricted area passes that comply with the
  requirements of sections 392 and 394, as applicable.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2006-269, s. 16
- SOR/2014-162, s. 100
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-384-20081215.html "Link to previous version of section 384"){.PITLink}
:::

#### [General]{.HTitleText3} {#h-711058 .Subheading}

- **[[385]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-385 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  holder of a restricted area pass or key that has been lost or stolen
  shall immediately report its loss or theft to the port administration
  or the operator of the marine facility.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} The port administration or operator of the marine
  facility shall immediately cancel a restricted area pass and any
  associated key upon being notified of its loss or theft.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2006-269, s. 16
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-385-20061102.html "Link to previous version of section 385"){.PITLink}
:::

- **[[386]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-386 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  port administration or an operator of a marine facility shall keep a
  record of

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the number of restricted area passes or keys issued
    and, for each pass, the name of the holder, the number of the pass
    or key, the date of issue, the period of validity, and, if
    applicable, the date of suspension or revocation; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} lost or stolen passes or keys.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} The port administration or operator of a marine
  facility shall ensure that the records referred to in subsection (1)
  are:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} kept for at least two years after the date they are
    made; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} available to the Minister on request.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2006-269, s. 17(F)
- SOR/2006-270, s. 8
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-386-20061102.html "Link to previous version of section 386"){.PITLink}
:::

**[[387]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-387 .sectionLabel}** A person shall not
provide false information for the purpose of obtaining a restricted area
pass or a key.

**[[388]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-388 .sectionLabel}** A person shall not use
a restricted area pass or a key except while in the performance of their
duties.

- **[[389]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-389 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  holder of a restricted area pass or a key shall return it to the
  marine facility operator or the person who issued it when

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the holder ceases to work at a marine facility; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the holder otherwise ceases to require the
    restricted area pass or the key.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} When a restricted area pass or a key is returned to
  an employer, the employer shall immediately give it to the marine
  facility operator or the person who issued it.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2006-269, s. 18
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-389-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 389"){.PITLink}
:::

**[[390]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-390 .sectionLabel}** A person shall
surrender on demand a restricted area pass or a key in their possession
to the marine facility operator, the person who issued it, a peace
officer or the Minister.

**[[391]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-391 .sectionLabel}** No person shall

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} loan or give a restricted area pass or a key that was
  issued to one person to another person;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} alter or otherwise modify a restricted area pass or a
  key;

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} have or use a restricted area pass or a key that was
  issued to another person;

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} use a counterfeit restricted area pass or key; or

- [(e)]{.lawlabel} make or reproduce a copy of a restricted area pass or
  key.

#### [Content]{.HTitleText3} {#h-711089 .Subheading}

- **[[392]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-392
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (2), a
  restricted area pass shall show the name, height and eye colour of the
  person to whom the pass has been issued, a clear photograph of the
  person's head and shoulders or other facial image and an expiry date
  that is not later than five years after the date of issue or, in the
  case of a restricted area pass issued to a person who holds a
  transportation security clearance, that is not later than the expiry
  date of the transportation security clearance.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} A temporary restricted area pass need not meet the
  requirements of subsection (1), but it shall bear a mark that clearly
  distinguishes it as a temporary pass.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2006-269, s. 19
- SOR/2014-162, s. 100
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-392-20081215.html "Link to previous version of section 392"){.PITLink}
:::

#### [Administration]{.HTitleText3} {#h-711095 .Subheading}

- **[[393]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-393 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  port administration may issue keys and restricted area passes, and
  keep an up-to-date list, by name and position, of the holders of
  transportation security clearances, on behalf of marine facility
  operators in the port.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} If a port administration administers restricted areas
  passes on behalf of marine facility operators in the port, a marine
  facility operator shall cooperate with the port administration and
  shall provide it with the information required.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2006-269, s. 20
- SOR/2014-162, s. 100
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-393-20081215.html "Link to previous version of section 393"){.PITLink}
:::

#### [Distinctive Mark --- Transportation Security Clearances]{.HTitleText3} {#h-711101 .Subheading}

::: HistoricalNote
\[

- SOR/2014-162, s. 100

\]
:::

**[[394]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-394 .sectionLabel}** Every restricted area
pass issued to a holder of a transportation security clearance shall
bear a mark that clearly distinguishes it from restricted area passes
issued to persons who are not transportation security clearance holders.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2006-269, s. 21
- SOR/2014-162, s. 100
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-394-20081215.html "Link to previous version of section 394"){.PITLink}
:::

## [PART 5]{.HLabel1}[Transportation Security Clearance]{.HTitleText1} {#h-711111 .Part}

::: HistoricalNote
\[

- SOR/2014-162, s. 100

\]
:::

**\[500 reserved\]**

### [Application]{.HTitleText2} {#h-711115 .Subheading}

- **[[501]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-501 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} This
  Part applies to the marine facilities set out in Part 1 of Schedule 1
  and to the port administrations for the ports at which the marine
  facilities are located.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} This Part also applies to every licensed ship's
  pilot, as defined in section 2 of the [Pilotage Act](/eng/acts/P-14),
  having the conduct of a vessel that interfaces with a marine facility
  set out in Part 1 of Schedule 1 or of a vessel in the waters of a port
  set out in Part 2 or 3 of that Schedule.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2006-269, s. 22
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-501-20061102.html "Link to previous version of section 501"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Operator of a Marine Facility]{.HTitleText2} {#h-711121 .Subheading}

**[[502]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-502 .sectionLabel}** The operator of a
marine facility and the port administration shall ensure that the
requirements of this Part are met.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2006-269, s. 22
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-502-20061103.html "Link to previous version of section 502"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Transportation Security Clearance]{.HTitleText2} {#h-711125 .Subheading}

::: HistoricalNote
\[

- SOR/2014-162, s. 100

\]
:::

**[[503]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-503 .sectionLabel}** Every person shall be a
holder of a transportation security clearance if they

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} require access to a restricted area two and cannot
  enter the area under any of paragraphs 380(1)(b) to (f) or subsection
  380(2) or (3);

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} are a licensed ship's pilot referred to in subsection
  501(2);

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} are a harbour master or wharfinger appointed under
  subsection 69(1) of the [Canada Marine Act](/eng/acts/C-6.7);

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} have security responsibilities, including authorized
  screening and security guard functions, at the marine facilities and
  port administrations for the ports referred to in subsection 501(1);

- [(e)]{.lawlabel} take applications for transportation security
  clearances and the applicants' fingerprints and facial images, which
  functions are performed on behalf of the Minister and for the purposes
  of this Part;

- [(f)]{.lawlabel} have access to a cruise ship that is interfacing with
  a restricted area two to provide services, supplies or equipment to
  the cruise ship or a member of the complement of the cruise ship;

- [(g)]{.lawlabel} could cause the failure of a preventive measure,
  delay the response to a security incident or adversely affect the
  recovery from a security incident as a result of being assigned or
  performing any of the following duties, responsibilities or functions:

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} access to security information at the marine
    facility or port,

  - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the supervision of marine facility operations,

  - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} the creation, alteration, control or maintenance
    of cargo documentation or crew or passenger lists by a person who

    - [(A)]{.lawlabel} is present at the marine facility or port, or

    - [(B)]{.lawlabel} has advance access to the documentation or lists,
      or

  - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} the planning or directing of the movement of cargo
    or containers at a container terminal, including their loading and
    unloading into and from vessels; or

- [(h)]{.lawlabel} are a seafarer who has submitted an application for a
  Seafarer's Identification Document.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2006-269, s. 22
- SOR/2014-162, ss. 47, 100
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-503-20081215.html "Link to previous version of section 503"){.PITLink}
:::

**[[504]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-504 .sectionLabel}** The holder of a
transportation security clearance who has been issued a restricted area
pass shall display the pass on their outer clothing and above their
waist, with their photograph or other facial image visible at all times
when they

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} perform the responsibilities, duties or functions for
  which a transportation security clearance is required; or

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} enter or remain in a restricted area two.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2006-269, s. 22
- SOR/2014-162, s. 100
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-504-20081215.html "Link to previous version of section 504"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Eligibility]{.HTitleText2} {#h-711150 .Subheading}

**[[505]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-505 .sectionLabel}** The following persons
may submit an application for a transportation security clearance:

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} any person who is required to obtain a transportation
  security clearance under these Regulations;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} any person applying for a job for which a
  transportation security clearance would be required under these
  Regulations; and

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} any seafarer who wishes to voluntarily submit an
  application for a transportation security clearance.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2006-269, s. 22
- SOR/2014-162, s. 100
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-505-20061102.html "Link to previous version of section 505"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Application Requirements]{.HTitleText2} {#h-711157 .Subheading}

- **[[506]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-506 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} In
  this section, [[common-law partner]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means any
  person who is cohabiting with the applicant in a relationship of a
  conjugal nature and has done so for a period of at least one year.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} An application for a transportation security
  clearance shall include the following information and documentation,
  to be used only for the purposes of sections 508 and 509:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the applicant's usual given name used, other given
    names, surname, all other names used and details of any name
    changes;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the applicant's date of birth, gender, height,
    weight, and eye and hair colour;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} if the applicant was born in Canada, the number and
    province of issue of their birth certificate, as well as the
    original of that certificate;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} if the applicant was born outside Canada, their
    place of birth, the port and date of entry, and, in the case of a
    naturalized Canadian or permanent resident, the number and the
    original of the applicable certificate issued under the [Citizenship
    Act](/eng/acts/C-29)or the [Immigration and Refugee Protection
    Act](/eng/acts/I-2.5);

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a foreign national, the original of
    any document that is evidence of their status;

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} the applicant's passport number, including the
    country of issue and expiry date, or an indication that the
    applicant does not have a passport;

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} the addresses of all locations at which the
    applicant resided during the five years preceding the application;

  - [(h)]{.lawlabel} an identification of the applicant's activities
    during the five years preceding the application, including the names
    and street addresses of the applicant's employers and any
    post-secondary educational institutions attended;

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the dates, destination and purpose of any travel of
    more than 90 days outside Canada or the United States, excluding
    travel for government business, during the five years preceding the
    application;

  - [(j)]{.lawlabel} the information referred to in subsection (3)
    respecting the applicant's spouse or common-law partner, any former
    spouses or common-law partners;

  - [(k)]{.lawlabel} the applicant's fingerprints, taken by or on behalf
    of the Minister;

  - [(l)]{.lawlabel} a facial image of the applicant for identification
    purposes, taken by or on behalf of the Minister;

  - [(m)]{.lawlabel} a statement signed by the marine facility operator
    or port administration certifying that the applicant requires or
    will require a transportation security clearance and specifying the
    reasons for that requirement; and

  - [(n)]{.lawlabel} a statement signed by the person responsible for
    taking the fingerprints of the applicant certifying that they have
    confirmed the identity of the applicant in accordance with paragraph
    384(3)(a) at the time of the taking of the fingerprints.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} The information required with respect to any of the
  persons referred to in paragraph (2)(j) shall be

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in the case of the spouse or common-law partner of
    the applicant, the following information:

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} their gender, full given name, surname and, if
      applicable, maiden name,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} their date and place of birth and, if
      applicable, date of death,

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} if born in Canada, the number and province of
      issue of their birth certificate,

    - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} if born outside Canada, their place of birth,
      their nationality and the port and date of entry into Canada, and

    - [(v)]{.lawlabel} their present address, if known; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in the case of former spouses and common-law
    partners with whom the relationship ended within the preceding five
    years, the information referred to in subparagraphs (a)(i), (ii) and
    (v).

- [(4)]{.lawlabel} The application for a transportation security
  clearance shall be valid only if signed by the applicant or, in the
  case of an applicant who is a minor under the laws of the province
  where they reside, by a parent or guardian or tutor.

- [(5)]{.lawlabel} Personal information that is provided in the
  application for the transportation security clearance and that
  resulting from the checks and verifications shall not, without the
  written consent of the individual to whom it relates, be disclosed by
  the Minister to the government of a foreign state except

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} where, in the opinion of the Minister, the public
    interest in disclosure clearly outweighs any invasion of privacy
    that could result from the disclosure; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} for the purpose of complying with a subpoena or
    warrant issued or order made by a court, person or body with
    jurisdiction to compel the production of information or for the
    purpose of complying with rules of court relating to the production
    of information.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2006-269, s. 22
- SOR/2014-162, s. 100
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-506-20061102.html "Link to previous version of section 506"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Submission of Application]{.HTitleText2} {#h-711189 .Subheading}

- **[[507]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-507
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (2), every
  application for a transportation security clearance shall be submitted
  on the form supplied by the Minister to the port administration at the
  port where the applicant works or is a candidate for work or to which
  the applicant otherwise requires access.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} If the port administration is not able to transmit
  the application in accordance with subsection (3), the application
  shall be submitted to an official of an office

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} managed by or for an airport authority and that is
    responsible for the control of the airport's passes, where the
    airport authority is able to transmit the application in accordance
    with a document that evidences the understanding to that effect with
    the Minister; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} operated by the Department of Transport.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} The port administration or official shall collect the
  applicant's information on behalf of the Minister and shall transmit
  it to the Minister in accordance with the document that evidences the
  understanding to that effect with the Minister.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2006-269, s. 22
- SOR/2014-162, s. 100
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-507-20061102.html "Link to previous version of section 507"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Clearance Process]{.HTitleText2} {#h-711198 .Subheading}

#### [Checks and Verifications]{.HTitleText3} {#h-711199 .Subheading}

**[[508]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-508 .sectionLabel}** On receipt of a fully
completed application for a transportation security clearance, the
Minister shall conduct the following checks and verifications for the
purpose of assessing whether an applicant poses a risk to the security
of marine transportation:

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} a criminal record check;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} a check of the relevant files of law enforcement
  agencies, including intelligence gathered for law enforcement
  purposes;

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} a Canadian Security Intelligence Service indices
  check and, if necessary, a Canadian Security Intelligence Service
  security assessment; and

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} a check of the applicant's immigration and
  citizenship status.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2006-269, s. 22
- SOR/2014-162, s. 100
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-508-20061102.html "Link to previous version of section 508"){.PITLink}
:::

#### [Minister's Decision]{.HTitleText3} {#h-711207 .Subheading}

**[[509]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-509 .sectionLabel}** The Minister may grant
a transportation security clearance if, in the opinion of the Minister,
the information provided by the applicant and that resulting from the
checks and verifications is verifiable and reliable and is sufficient
for the Minister to determine, by an evaluation of the following
factors, to what extent the applicant poses a risk to the security of
marine transportation:

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the relevance of any criminal convictions to the
  security of marine transportation, including a consideration of the
  type, circumstances and seriousness of the offence, the number and
  frequency of convictions, the length of time between offences, the
  date of the last offence and the sentence or disposition;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} whether it is known or there are reasonable grounds
  to suspect that the applicant

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} is or has been involved in, or contributes or has
    contributed to, activities directed toward or in support of the
    misuse of the transportation infrastructure to commit criminal
    offences or the use of acts of violence against persons or property,
    taking into account the relevance of those activities to the
    security of marine transportation,

  - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} is or has been a member of a terrorist group
    within the meaning of subsection 83.01(1) of the [Criminal
    Code](/eng/acts/C-46), or is or has been involved in, or contributes
    or has contributed to, the activities of such a group,

  - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} is or has been a member of a criminal
    organization as defined in subsection 467.1(1) of the [Criminal
    Code](/eng/acts/C-46), or participates or has participated in, or
    contributes or has contributed to, the activities of such a group as
    referred to in subsection 467.11(1) of the [Criminal
    Code](/eng/acts/C-46) taking into account the relevance of these
    factors to the security of marine transportation,

  - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} is or has been a member of an organization that is
    known to be involved in or to contribute to --- or in respect of
    which there are reasonable grounds to suspect involvement in or
    contribution to --- activities directed toward or in support of the
    threat of or the use of, acts of violence against persons or
    property, or is or has been involved in, or is contributing to or
    has contributed to, the activities of such a group, taking into
    account the relevance of those factors to the security of marine
    transportation, or

  - [(v)]{.lawlabel} is or has been associated with an individual who is
    known to be involved in or to contribute to --- or in respect of
    whom there are reasonable grounds to suspect involvement in or
    contribution to --- activities referred to in subparagraph (i), or
    is a member of an organization or group referred to in any of
    subparagraphs (ii) to (iv), taking into account the relevance of
    those factors to the security of marine transportation;

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} whether there are reasonable grounds to suspect that
  the applicant is in a position in which there is a risk that they be
  suborned to commit an act or to assist or abet any person to commit an
  act that might constitute a risk to marine transportation security;

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} whether the applicant has had a restricted area pass
  for a marine facility, port or aerodrome removed for cause; and

- [(e)]{.lawlabel} whether the applicant has filed fraudulent, false or
  misleading information relating to their application for a
  transportation security clearance.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2006-269, s. 22
- SOR/2014-162, s. 100
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-509-20061102.html "Link to previous version of section 509"){.PITLink}
:::

**[[510]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-510 .sectionLabel}** If a criminal charge is
outstanding against the applicant that could, if the applicant were
convicted, be considered by the Minister under paragraph 509(a), the
Minister may decline to process the application until the charge has
been disposed of by the courts, in which case the Minister shall advise
the applicant in writing.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2006-269, s. 22
:::

- **[[511]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-511 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If
  the Minister intends to refuse to grant a transportation security
  clearance, the Minister shall advise the applicant in writing to that
  effect.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} The notice shall set out the basis for the Minister's
  intention and fix a period of time for the applicant to make written
  representations to the Minister, which period of time shall start on
  the day on which the notice is served or sent and shall be not less
  than 20 days from that day.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} The Minister shall not refuse to grant a
  transportation security clearance until the written representations
  have been received and considered or before the period of time fixed
  in the notice has expired, whichever comes first. The Minister shall
  advise the applicant in writing of any refusal.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2006-269, s. 22
- SOR/2014-162, s. 100
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-511-20061102.html "Link to previous version of section 511"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Validity of Clearances]{.HTitleText2} {#h-711230 .Subheading}

- **[[512]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-512 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  Minister shall establish a period of validity for a transportation
  security clearance in accordance with the level of risk posed by the
  applicant determined under section 509, but the period shall not
  exceed five years.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} If the validity period is less than five years, the
  Minister may extend the period to a total of five years if the
  Minister determines under section 509 that the holder does not pose a
  risk to the security of marine transportation.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} If a transportation security clearance is suspended
  and subsequently reinstated, the end of the validity period remains
  the same as that established at the time of issuance.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2006-269, s. 22
- SOR/2014-162, s. 100
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-512-20061102.html "Link to previous version of section 512"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Suspension, Reinstatement or Cancellation of Clearances]{.HTitleText2} {#h-711237 .Subheading}

**[[513]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-513 .sectionLabel}** A marine facility
operator or port administration shall immediately notify the Minister in
writing when the holder of a transportation security clearance is no
longer required by these Regulations to be the holder of a
transportation security clearance.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2006-269, s. 22
- SOR/2014-162, s. 100
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-513-20061102.html "Link to previous version of section 513"){.PITLink}
:::

**[[514]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-514 .sectionLabel}** A marine facility
operator or port administration shall immediately notify the Minister in
writing when they suspend or cancel, for security reasons, a restricted
area pass issued to a holder of a transportation security clearance.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2006-269, s. 22
- SOR/2014-162, s. 100
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-514-20061102.html "Link to previous version of section 514"){.PITLink}
:::

- **[[515]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-515 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  Minister may suspend a transportation security clearance on receipt of
  information that could change the Minister's determination made under
  section 509.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} Immediately after suspending a transportation
  security clearance, the Minister shall advise the holder in writing of
  the suspension.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} The notice shall set out the basis for the suspension
  and shall fix a period of time for the holder to make written
  representations to the Minister, which period of time shall start on
  the day on which the notice is served or sent and shall be not less
  than 20 days from that day.

- [(4)]{.lawlabel} The Minister may reinstate the transportation
  security clearance if the Minister determines under section 509 that
  the holder does not pose a risk to marine transportation security.

- [(5)]{.lawlabel} The Minister may cancel the transportation security
  clearance if the Minister determines under section 509 that the holder
  may pose a risk to marine transportation security or that the
  transportation security clearance is no longer required. The Minister
  shall advise the holder in writing of any cancellation.

- [(6)]{.lawlabel} The Minister shall not cancel a transportation
  security clearance until the written representations have been
  received and considered or before the time period fixed in the notice
  has expired, whichever comes first.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2006-269, s. 22
- SOR/2014-162, s. 100
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-515-20061102.html "Link to previous version of section 515"){.PITLink}
:::

### [New Applications]{.HTitleText2} {#h-711253 .Subheading}

**[[516]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-516 .sectionLabel}** If the Minister refuses
to grant or cancels a transportation security clearance, an applicant
may submit a new application only if

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} a period of five years has elapsed after the day of
  the refusal or cancellation; or

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} a change has occurred in the circumstances that led
  to the refusal or cancellation.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2006-269, s. 22
- SOR/2014-162, s. 100
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-516-20061102.html "Link to previous version of section 516"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Reconsideration]{.HTitleText2} {#h-711259 .Subheading}

- **[[517]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-517 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} An
  applicant or a holder may request that the Minister reconsider a
  decision to refuse to grant or to cancel a transportation security
  clearance within 30 days after the day of the service or sending of
  the notice advising them of the decision.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} The request shall be in writing and shall set out the
  following:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the decision that is the subject of the request;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the grounds for the request, including any new
    information that the applicant or holder wishes the Minister to
    consider; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the name, address, and telephone and facsimile
    numbers of the applicant or holder.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} On receipt of a request made in accordance with this
  section, the Minister, in order to determine the matter in a fair,
  informal and expeditious manner, shall give the applicant or holder

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} where the situation warrants, the opportunity to
    make representations orally or in any other manner; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in any other case, a reasonable opportunity to make
    written representations.

- [(4)]{.lawlabel} After representations have been made or a reasonable
  opportunity to do so has been provided, the Minister shall reconsider
  the decision in accordance with section 509 and shall subsequently
  confirm or change the decision.

- [(5)]{.lawlabel} The Minister may engage the services of persons with
  appropriate expertise in security matters to advise the Minister.

- [(6)]{.lawlabel} The Minister shall advise the applicant or holder in
  writing of the decision made following the reconsideration.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2006-269, s. 22
- SOR/2014-162, s. 100
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-517-20061102.html "Link to previous version of section 517"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Notices]{.HTitleText2} {#h-711274 .Subheading}

**[[518]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-518 .sectionLabel}** The Minister shall send
to the person at their last known address, by personal service or
registered mail, any notices to be given under this Part.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2006-269, s. 22
:::

### [False Applications]{.HTitleText2} {#h-711278 .Subheading}

**[[519]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-519 .sectionLabel}** No person shall
knowingly file with the Minister a fraudulent application or an
application containing false or misleading information in order to
obtain a transportation security clearance.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2006-269, s. 22
- SOR/2014-162, s. 100
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-519-20061102.html "Link to previous version of section 519"){.PITLink}
:::

**\[520 to 599 reserved\]**

## [PART 6]{.HLabel1}[Contravention of Provisions Designated as Violations]{.HTitleText1} {#h-711283 .Part}

**\[600 reserved\]**

### [Violations]{.HTitleText2} {#h-711285 .Subheading}

- **[[601]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-601 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  contravention of a provision of the Act set out in column 1 of an item
  of Schedule 2 is designated a violation that may be proceeded with in
  accordance with sections 33 to 46, 49 and 50 of the Act.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} For each violation designated under subsection (1)

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the penalty or range of penalties for

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} an individual is set out in column 2, and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} a corporation is set out in column 3; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} if an "X" appears in column 4, the violation
    constitutes a separate violation for each day on which it is
    continued.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2006-270, s. 9
:::

- **[[602]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-602 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  contravention of a provision of these Regulations set out in column 1
  of an item of Schedule 3 is designated a violation that may be
  proceeded with in accordance with sections 33 to 46, 49 and 50 of the
  Act.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} For each violation designated under subsection (1),

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the penalty or range of penalties for

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} an individual is set out in column 2, and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} a corporation is set out in column 3; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} if an "X" appears in column 4, the violation
    constitutes a separate violation for each day on which it is
    continued.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2006-270, s. 9
:::

## [PART 7]{.HLabel1}[Notice and Service of Documents]{.HTitleText1} {#h-711304 .Part}

**\[700 reserved\]**

### [Service of Documents]{.HTitleText2} {#h-711306 .Subheading}

- **[[701]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-701 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} This
  section applies to the following documents:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a notice of violation referred to in paragraph
    33(1)(b) of the Act;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a notice of compliance referred to in section 35 of
    the Act;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} a notice of default referred to in subsection 36(1)
    of the Act;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} a notice of refusal to issue a certificate of
    proficiency referred to in subsection 802(2); and

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} a notice of suspension or cancellation referred to
    in subsection 14(2) or 805(1).

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} A document may be served on an individual by

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} giving a copy to the individual;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} giving a copy to someone who appears to be an adult
    member of the same household at the last known address or usual
    place of residence of the individual; or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} sending a copy by registered mail or courier to the
    last known address or usual place of residence of the individual or
    by facsimile transmission to the last facsimile number provided.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} A document may be served on a person other than an
  individual by

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} giving a copy to one of the person's officers or
    agents at the person's head office or place of business; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} sending a copy by registered mail or courier to the
    head office or place of business of the person or their agent or by
    facsimile transmission to the last facsimile number provided.

- [(4)]{.lawlabel} A document that is served by facsimile transmission
  shall include a cover page setting out

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the name and address and telephone and facsimile
    numbers of the sender;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the date and time of transmission;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the total number of pages transmitted, including
    the cover page; and

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the name and telephone number of a person to
    contact in the event of transmission problems, if they differ from
    those provided for the sender.

- [(5)]{.lawlabel} A document that is served by

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} registered mail is deemed to have been served on
    the fourth day after the day on which it was sent; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} facsimile transmission is deemed to have been
    served on the day after the day on which it was sent.

- [(6)]{.lawlabel} Proof of service of a document may be made by showing

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a document transmitted by facsimile,
    a proof of transmission produced by the facsimile machine that sets
    out the date and time of transmission; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in any other case, an acknowledgement of service
    signed by or on behalf of the person served, specifying the date and
    place of service.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2006-270, s. 9
- SOR/2014-162, s. 48
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-701-20071102.html "Link to previous version of section 701"){.PITLink}
:::

## [PART 8]{.HLabel1}[Certification]{.HTitleText1} {#h-711334 .Part}

::: HistoricalNote
\[

- SOR/2014-162, s. 49

\]
:::

### [Interpretation]{.HTitleText2} {#h-711337 .Subheading}

::: HistoricalNote
\[

- SOR/2014-162, s. 50(F)

\]
:::

- **[[800]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-800
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, SOR/2014-162, s.
  51\]]{.Repealed}

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}References in STCW Code

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} For the purpose of interpreting tables A-VI/5,
  A-VI/6-1 and A-VI/6-2 of the STCW Code,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} "ship" shall be read to mean "vessel";

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} "measures and procedures" shall be read to mean
    "requirements and procedures";

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} "port facility" shall be read to mean "marine
    facility";

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} "ship/port interface" shall be read to mean
    "vessel/marine facility interface"; and

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} "designating and monitoring restricted areas" shall
    be read to mean "establishing and monitoring restricted areas".

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}References in French version of STCW
  Code

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} For the purpose of interpreting tables A-VI/5,
  A-VI/6-1 and A-VI/6-2 of the French version of the STCW Code,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} "audits" shall be read to mean
    "[vérifications]{.otherLang lang="fr"}"; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} "[zones d'accès restreints]{.otherLang lang="fr"}"
    shall be read to mean "[zones réglementées]{.otherLang lang="fr"}".

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2007-275, s. 6
- SOR/2014-162, s. 51
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-800-20080101.html "Link to previous version of section 800"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Issuance of a Certificate of Proficiency]{.HTitleText2} {#h-711355 .Subheading}

::: HistoricalNote
\[

- SOR/2014-162, s. 52

\]
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Requirements

- **[[801]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-801 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  Minister shall issue a certificate of proficiency to a person if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the Minister receives certification from a
    recognized institution that the person has successfully completed an
    approved training course;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a certificate of proficiency as a
    ship security officer, the person has at least 12 months of sea
    service, calculated in accordance with sections 115, 116 and 118 of
    the [Marine Personnel Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-2007-115);

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the person is at least 18 years of age;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the person provides proof that he or she is a
    Canadian citizen or a permanent resident as defined in subsection
    2(1) of the [Immigration and Refugee Protection
    Act](/eng/acts/I-2.5);

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} the person meets the standards of medical fitness
    set out in Division 8 of Part 2 of the [Marine Personnel
    Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-2007-115) and has been issued a
    medical certificate or a provisional medical certificate under that
    Division; and

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} the person meets the standards of competence
    required by these Regulations for the performance of the duties
    associated with the capacities identified in the certificate of
    proficiency.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}References to "qualifying service"

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} For the purpose of paragraph (1)(b), the references
  in subsection 115(3) and section 116 of the [Marine Personnel
  Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-2007-115) to "qualifying service"
  shall be read as references to "sea service".

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Alternative before July 1, 2014

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The Minister shall issue a certificate of proficiency
  as a member of the vessel personnel with security responsibilities to
  a person who commenced sea service before January 1, 2012 if the
  person

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} provides the Minister, no later than June 30, 2014,
    with a testimonial indicating that the person has undertaken the
    tasks, duties and responsibilities listed in column 1 of table
    A-VI/6-2 of the STCW Code;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} has acquired, during the three years preceding
    January 1, 2014, a total of at least six months of sea service,
    calculated in accordance with sections 115, 116 and 118 of the
    [Marine Personnel Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-2007-115);

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} is at least 18 years of age;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} provides the Minister with proof that the person is
    a Canadian citizen or a permanent resident as defined in subsection
    2(1) of the [Immigration and Refugee Protection
    Act](/eng/acts/I-2.5); and

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} meets the standards of medical fitness set out in
    Division 8 of Part 2 of the [Marine Personnel
    Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-2007-115) and has been issued a
    medical certificate or a provisional medical certificate under that
    Division.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Alternative before July 1, 2014

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} The Minister shall issue a certificate of proficiency
  as a member of the vessel personnel without security responsibilities
  to a person who commenced sea service before January 1, 2012 if the
  person

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} provides the Minister, no later than June 30, 2014,
    with a testimonial indicating that the person has undertaken the
    tasks, duties and responsibilities listed in column 1 of table
    A-VI/6-1 of the STCW Code;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} has acquired, during the three years preceding
    January 1, 2014, a total of at least six months of sea service,
    calculated in accordance with sections 115, 116 and 118 of the
    [Marine Personnel Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-2007-115);

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} is at least 18 years of age;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} provides the Minister with proof that the person is
    a Canadian citizen or a permanent resident as defined in subsection
    2(1) of the [Immigration and Refugee Protection
    Act](/eng/acts/I-2.5); and

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} meets the standards of medical fitness set out in
    Division 8 of Part 2 of the [Marine Personnel
    Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-2007-115) and has been issued a
    medical certificate or a provisional medical certificate under that
    Division.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2007-275, s. 6
- SOR/2014-162, s. 53
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-801-20080101.html "Link to previous version of section 801"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Refusal to issue

- **[[802]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-802
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Despite section 801, the Minister
  shall refuse to issue a certificate of proficiency to a person if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the person has not met the requirements for the
    issuance of the certificate; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the Minister has reasonable grounds to believe that

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the person has acted fraudulently or improperly
      or has misrepresented a material fact, or

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the public interest and, in particular, the
      record of the person warrant it.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notice of refusal to issue

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} If the Minister refuses to issue a certificate of
  proficiency to a person, the Minister shall notify the person of the
  refusal. The notice shall include

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the grounds for the refusal; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the address at which, and the date, being 30 days
    after the day on which the notice is given, on or before which, the
    person may file a request for a review of the refusal.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2007-275, s. 6
- SOR/2014-162, s. 54
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-802-20080101.html "Link to previous version of section 802"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Approved Training Course]{.HTitleText2} {#h-711398 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Approval by Minister

- **[[803]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-803 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  Minister shall approve a course as an approved training course if the
  Minister determines that it

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} provides participants with the training necessary
    to undertake the tasks, duties and responsibilities listed in column
    1 of tables A-VI/5, A-VI/6-1 and A-V1/6-2 of the STCW Code and to
    acquire the knowledge listed in column 2 of those tables; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} uses the methods and criteria listed in columns 3
    and 4, respectively, of tables A-VI/5, A-VI/6-1 and A-V1/6-2 of the
    STCW Code to evaluate the participants' competence to undertake the
    tasks, duties and responsibilities referred to in paragraph (a).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Revocation

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The Minister shall revoke the approval of a course as
  an approved training course if, after the results of an independent
  evaluation made in accordance with section A-I/8.3 of the STCW Code
  are brought to the attention of the recognized institution that is
  providing the course, timely action is not taken to correct any
  deficiencies.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2007-275, s. 6
- SOR/2014-162, s. 55
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-803-20080101.html "Link to previous version of section 803"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Recognized Institution]{.HTitleText2} {#h-711408 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Designation by Minister

- **[[803.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-803.1
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The Minister shall designate a
  training institution as a recognized institution if the Minister is
  satisfied that the training institution administers, in accordance
  with the established practices and the requirements of the domestic
  and international marine industry, approved training courses for the
  purpose of providing the training necessary to obtain a certificate of
  proficiency issued under this Part.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Monitoring

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The approved training courses shall be monitored
  continuously through a quality management system that conforms to
  Regulation I/8 of the STCW Convention.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Revocation

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The Minister shall revoke the designation of a
  recognized institution if, after informing the recognized institution
  that the results of an evaluation conducted by the Minister indicate
  that the requirements of subsection (1) or (2) are not being met, the
  Minister is satisfied that timely action has not been taken to correct
  the deficiencies.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2014-162, s. 56
:::

### [Suspension and Cancellation]{.HTitleText2} {#h-711418 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Suspension or cancellation

- **[[804]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-804 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  Minister shall suspend or cancel a person's certificate of proficiency
  if the Minister has reasonable grounds to believe that

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the certificate was obtained by any fraudulent or
    improper means or a misrepresentation of any material fact;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the public interest warrants it, particularly in
    view of the person's record; or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the person's performance of the duties associated
    with the capacities identified in the certificate constitutes, or is
    likely to constitute, an immediate threat to marine transportation
    security.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Cancellation

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The Minister shall cancel a person's certificate of
  proficiency if the Minister determines that the person is no longer a
  Canadian citizen or a permanent resident as defined in subsection 2(1)
  of the [Immigration and Refugee Protection Act](/eng/acts/I-2.5).

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2007-275, s. 6
- SOR/2014-162, s. 57
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-804-20080101.html "Link to previous version of section 804"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notice of suspension or cancellation

- **[[805]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-805 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If
  the Minister suspends or cancels a person's certificate of
  proficiency, the Minister shall notify the person of the suspension or
  cancellation. The notice shall include

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the grounds for the suspension or cancellation; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the address at which, and the date, being 30 days
    after the day on which the notice is given, on or before which, the
    person may file a request for a review of the suspension or
    cancellation.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}When effective

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The suspension or cancellation shall not be effective
  earlier than

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a suspension or cancellation on the
    grounds referred to in paragraph 804(1)(a) or (b), the 30th day
    after the day on which the person receives the notice; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a suspension or cancellation on the
    grounds referred to in paragraph 804(1)(c) or subsection 804(2),
    when the person receives the notice.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}When notice is considered received

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Notice is considered to be received on the earlier of

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} when the person receives the notice, and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} when any vessel of which the person is the master
    or a crew member receives the notice.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2007-275, s. 6
- SOR/2014-162, s. 58
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-805-20080101.html "Link to previous version of section 805"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Reviews and Appeals]{.HTitleText2} {#h-711444 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Jurisdiction of Tribunal

**[[806]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-806 .sectionLabel}** The Tribunal has
jurisdiction in respect of reviews and appeals under sections 807 to
810.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2007-275, s. 6
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Request for review

- **[[807]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-807 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  person who is notified under subsection 802(2) or 805(1) may, on or
  before the date specified in the notice or within any further time
  that the Tribunal on application allows, file with the Tribunal a
  written request for a review of the refusal, suspension or
  cancellation.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Effect of request

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A request for a review does not operate as a stay of
  the refusal, suspension or cancellation.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} On application in writing by a person whose
  certificate of proficiency was suspended or cancelled on any of the
  grounds referred to in paragraph 804(1)(c) or subsection 804(2), a
  member of the Tribunal assigned for the purpose may, after giving
  notice to the Minister and after considering any representations made
  by the parties, grant a stay of the suspension or cancellation until
  the review is completed if he or she determines that granting a stay
  would not constitute a threat to marine transportation security.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2007-275, s. 6
- SOR/2014-162, s. 59
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-807-20080101.html "Link to previous version of section 807"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Time and place for review

- **[[808]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-808 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} On
  receipt of a request filed under section 807, the Tribunal shall
  appoint a time and place for the review and shall notify the Minister
  and the person who filed the request of the time and place in writing.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Determination

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The assigned member of the Tribunal shall confirm the
  refusal, suspension or cancellation or refer the matter back to the
  Minister for reconsideration.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2007-275, s. 6
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Right of appeal

- **[[809]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-809
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Within 30 days after the day on
  which a determination is made under subsection 808(2), the certificate
  holder may appeal the determination to the Tribunal.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Loss of right of appeal

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A party who does not appear at a review hearing is
  not entitled to appeal a determination unless they establish that
  there was sufficient reason to justify their absence.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Disposition of appeal

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The appeal panel of the Tribunal assigned to hear the
  appeal shall dismiss it or refer the matter back to the Minister for
  reconsideration.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2007-275, s. 6
- SOR/2014-162, s. 60
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-809-20080101.html "Link to previous version of section 809"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Decision to remain in effect pending
reconsideration

**[[810]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-810 .sectionLabel}** If a matter is referred
back to the Minister for reconsideration under subsection 808(2) or
809(3), a suspension or cancellation remains in effect until the
reconsideration is concluded. However, the member who made the
determination or the appeal panel, after considering any representations
made by the parties, may grant a stay of the suspension or cancellation
until the reconsideration is concluded if the member or panel determines
that granting a stay would not constitute a threat to marine
transportation security.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2007-275, s. 6
:::

### [General]{.HTitleText2} {#h-711478 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Possession of certificate

**[[811]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-811 .sectionLabel}** No person shall possess
a certificate of proficiency except the person to whom it was issued or
the person's representative.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2007-275, s. 6
- SOR/2014-162, s. 61
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-811-20080101.html "Link to previous version of section 811"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Production of certificate

**[[812]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-812 .sectionLabel}** The holder of a
certificate of proficiency shall produce it on the Minister's request.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2007-275, s. 6
- SOR/2014-162, s. 61
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-812-20080101.html "Link to previous version of section 812"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Return of certificate

**[[813]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-813 .sectionLabel}** If a certificate of
proficiency is suspended or cancelled, the person to whom it was issued
shall return it to the Minister on request.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2007-275, s. 6
- SOR/2014-162, s. 61
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-813-20080101.html "Link to previous version of section 813"){.PITLink}
:::

**\[814 to 899 reserved\]**

:::::: section
::::: {#711537 .Schedule}
::: {}
## [SCHEDULE 4]{.scheduleLabel}[(Subsections 1(1) and (4))]{.OriginatingRef}[Certain Dangerous Cargoes (CDCs)]{.scheduleTitleText}  {#h-711538 .scheduleLabel}
:::

- [1]{.lawlabel} All explosives of Class 1.1, 1.2 and 1.5.

- [2]{.lawlabel} The following gases of Class 2.1, when carried in a
  large means of containment or in such a quantity as to require an
  emergency response assistance plan (ERAP) under section 7.1 of the
  [Transportation of Dangerous Goods
  Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-2001-286):

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} UN1035, UN1086, UN1971 and UN1972; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} UN1010, UN1011, UN1012, UN1032, UN1036, UN1037,
    UN1060, UN1063, UN1077, UN1962 and UN1978, except when carried as
    CDC residue.

- [3]{.lawlabel} All gases of Class 2.3, when carried in a large means
  of containment or in such a quantity as to require an ERAP under
  section 7.1 of the [Transportation of Dangerous Goods
  Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-2001-286).

- [4]{.lawlabel} The following flammable liquids of Class 3, when
  carried in a large means of containment or in such a quantity as to
  require an ERAP under section 7.1 of the [Transportation of Dangerous
  Goods Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-2001-286), except when carried
  as CDC residue:

  UN1089 and UN1280.

- [5]{.lawlabel} The following oxidizing substances of Class 5.1, when
  carried in a large means of containment or in such a quantity as to
  require an ERAP under section 7.1 of the [Transportation of Dangerous
  Goods Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-2001-286):

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} UN1479 and UN3139; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} UN1942, UN2067 and UN3375, except when carried as
    CDC residue.

- [6]{.lawlabel} The following liquids of Class 6.1 (either primary or
  subsidiary class) that are toxic by inhalation, when carried in a
  large means of containment or in such a quantity as to require an ERAP
  under section 7.1 of the [Transportation of Dangerous Goods
  Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-2001-286):

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} UN1051, UN1052, UN1092, UN1163, UN1182, UN1185,
    UN1238, UN1239, UN1244, UN1251, UN1259, UN1380, UN1510, UN1560,
    UN1569, UN1580, UN1583, UN1595, UN1613, UN1614, UN1647, UN1670,
    UN1672, UN1695, UN1722, UN1744, UN1745, UN1746, UN1752, UN1809,
    UN1892, UN1994, UN2032, UN2232, UN2285, UN2334, UN2337, UN2382,
    UN2407, UN2438, UN2474, UN2477, UN2478, UN2480, UN2481, UN2482,
    UN2483, UN2484, UN2485, UN2486, UN2487, UN2488, UN2521, UN2605,
    UN2606, UN2644, UN2646, UN2668, UN2740, UN2742, UN2743, UN2809,
    UN3023, UN3246, UN3275, UN3276, UN3278, UN3279, UN3280, UN3281
    UN3294, UN3381, UN3382, UN3383, UN3384, UN3385, UN3386, UN3387,
    UN3388, UN3389, UN3390, UN3488, UN3489, UN3490, UN3491, UN3494 and
    UN3495; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} UN1098, UN1135, UN1143, UN1541, UN1605, UN1831,
    UN2983 and UN3079, except when carried as CDC residue.

- [7]{.lawlabel} Radioactive substances of Class 7, if the quantity in a
  single means of containment exceeds the least of

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} 3,000 times the A~1~ value, as defined in
    subsection 1(1) of the [Packaging and Transport of Nuclear
    Substances Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-2000-208), of the
    radionuclides,

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} 3,000 times the A~2~ value, as defined in
    subsection 1(1) of those Regulations, of the radionuclides, and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} 1,000 TBq (27,000 Ci).

- [8]{.lawlabel} The following corrosive substance of Class 8, when
  carried in a large means of containment or in such a quantity as to
  require an ERAP under section 7.1 of the [Transportation of Dangerous
  Goods Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-2001-286), except when carried
  as CDC residue:

  UN1754.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2014-162, s. 99
